> 176:   let content = "";
> 177:   if (existsSync(giPath)) {
> 178:     content = readFileSync(giPath, "utf8");

> 182:     .some((line) => line.trim() === pattern);
> 183:   if (present) return;
> 184:   const prefix = content === "" || content.endsWith("\n") ? "" : "\n";

> 7: /** Extract a PT-BR error message from a Supabase Edge Function non-2xx response. */
> 8: export async function edgeErrorMessage(error: unknown, fallback: string): Promise<string> {
> 9:   const ctx = (error as { context?: { json?: () => Promise<{ error?: string; message?: string; action?: string }> } })?.context;
> 10:   try {
> 11:     const body = await ctx?.json?.();
> 12:     // Ordem = do mais humano ao mais cru: `action` (o que FAZER, ex.: "Configure suas credenciais…"),
> 13:     // depois `message` (a frase pt-BR que a fn escreveu), e só então `error` — que na maioria das fns
> 14:     // é um CÓDIGO de máquina ("video_submit_failed") e vira um toast ilegível se vazar sozinho.
> 15:     if (body?.action) return body.action;
> 16:     if (body?.message) return body.message;
> 17:     if (body?.error) return body.error;
> 18:   } catch { /* response body was not JSON */ }
> 19:   return (error as { message?: string })?.message || fallback;
> 20: }

> 1: import { useMemo } from 'react';
> 2: import { useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
> 3: import * as THREE from 'three';
> 4: import { useUnifiedCanvasStore } from '@/store/useUnifiedCanvasStore';
> 5: 
> 6: // Camera fly-to rig (FR-047 / phase 3.6): eases the OrbitControls target (and the
> 7: // camera itself, keeping its current bearing at a fixed close-orbit distance)
> 8: // toward store.cameraTarget, then clears it on arrival. Runs inside the Canvas;
> 9: // requires <OrbitControls makeDefault /> so useThree exposes the controls.
> 10: // getState() inside useFrame — no React subscription churn per frame (Error #185 guard).
> 11: 
> 12: const FLY_DISTANCE = 6; // close-orbit distance on arrival
> 13: const ARRIVE_EPS = 0.08;
> 14: 
> 15: type OrbitControlsLike = { target: THREE.Vector3; update: () => void } | null;
> 16: 
> 17: export function CameraRig() {
> 18:   const controls = useThree((s) => s.controls) as OrbitControlsLike;
> 19:   // Hoisted temp vectors — zero allocations inside the frame loop.
> 20:   const tmp = useMemo(() => ({ target: new THREE.Vector3(), desired: new THREE.Vector3(), dir: new THREE.Vector3() }), []);
> 21: 
> 22:   useFrame(({ camera }) => {
> 23:     const { cameraTarget, setCameraTarget } = useUnifiedCanvasStore.getState();
> 24:     if (!cameraTarget || !controls) return;
> 25:     tmp.target.set(cameraTarget[0], cameraTarget[1], cameraTarget[2]);
> 26: 
> 27:     controls.target.lerp(tmp.target, 0.09);
> 28:     // Desired camera spot: FLY_DISTANCE away from the node along the current view bearing.
> 29:     tmp.dir.copy(camera.position).sub(controls.target);
> 30:     const len = tmp.dir.length();
> 31:     if (len > 1e-4) tmp.dir.multiplyScalar(1 / len);
> 32:     tmp.desired.copy(tmp.target).addScaledVector(tmp.dir, FLY_DISTANCE);
> 33:     camera.position.lerp(tmp.desired, 0.07);
> 34:     controls.update();
> 35: 
> 36:     if (controls.target.distanceTo(tmp.target) < ARRIVE_EPS) setCameraTarget(null);
> 37:   });
> 38: 
> 39:   return null;
> 40: }
> 41: 
> The above content shows the entire, complete file contents of the requested file.
